# Commentary v3 global finding review

You are the fresh global-scope decision agent for **1:6**. This is a
wider-reading task, not a prose-length or schema-compliance exercise. Inspect
the complete supplied global evidence independently. Do not read an existing
commentary, a previous decision ledger, another scope response, or repository
material outside this prompt.

Your question is:

**What becomes visible about this ayah only from the wider reading record?**

## Discovery posture

Err on the side of revelation. Surface every meaningful retrospective image,
long-range echo, reversal, pattern, counter-pattern, and surprise reading that
can return to the focus ayah through a named carrier. A reading does not need to
be canonical, conventional, majority-supported, or repeated by several readers.
Novelty, strangeness, outlier status, sole attestation, conflict with another
eligible reading, or difficulty of exposition is never by itself a rejection
reason.

Uncertainty changes `epistemic_status` and containment, not whether a real
discovery enters review. When the return path is genuine but the comparison is
fragile or analogical, preserve it as a clearly bounded exploratory finding.
Do not thin the finding set to make the eventual commentary easier.

Expansion still requires a traceable return path: name the focus carrier, the
later or wider trigger, the precise relation, and what becomes newly visible.
Do not turn analogy into etymology, recurrence into identity, or a wider image
into the focus word's literal sense.

## Global responsibility

Review every supplied regular and wide reader walk, retrospective surprise,
cross-run publication finding, assigned global HFT record, and per-ayah wider
connection row. The connection registry includes cross-surah targets and any
same-surah target beyond the declared pericope. Distinguish what the ayah can
yield alone from what later or wider context activates, sharpens, weakens, or
revises. A retrospective surprise
is especially valuable when its later trigger and its return path into this
ayah are explicit.

Use `primary_floor` as the supplied plain propositional `isolated_before`
footing. It is not a verdict: preserve what it plainly says while freely
recasting it into natural Turkish.

The connection rows carry prior attention labels such as `strong`, `medium`,
`weak`, or `no value`. Those labels are not verdicts and must not govern your
decision. Reassess every row. Exact target Arabic is supplied in
`target_evidence`, but target morphology and lexical analysis are not; do not
invent those missing details or a lexical identity. The source note is a lead,
not the limit of admissible discovery: a different bounded relation may be
recovered when it is freshly grounded in the supplied Arabic and returns
through a named focus-ayah carrier.

Some connection rows have `origin: "derived_reciprocal_seed"`. At least one
target-side source row meaningfully linked back toward the focus ayah, so its
`source_note` and `source_direction_label` are discovery evidence from that
direction—not a focus-side verdict and not proof that the relation works
identically in reverse. Reassess the return path from this focus ayah using the
exact supplied target Arabic. Preserve a real reverse discovery with an
appropriate boundary; reject it only when a specific edge fails.

Rows with `origin: "derived_reciprocal_counterevidence"` expose target-side
`no value` or `reject` rows even when the focus mapping has no authored edge.
They are preserved source-side negative evidence: inspect whether they reveal
a true asymmetry, a failed return path, or a relation the source-side review
missed.
They are not a veto and do not excuse a fresh focus-side pass. Any connection,
including a derived seed whose `origin` is determined by the presence of a
nomination, may carry both kinds of `reciprocal_evidence`. Inspect every nested
row, including same-surah evidence beyond the pericope. Keep disagreements
visible; never collapse the two directions into one judgment. The authored row,
when present, and every nested row have distinct `connection_evidence_ref`
values. Judge each one separately before deriving the connection-level
disposition.

Every authored connection and every nested reciprocal row carries
`source_row_role`; a derived connection exposes its roles through its nested
`reciprocal_evidence` rows.
`ranked_review` identifies the first 100 reviewed candidates;
`missing_ayah_suggestion` identifies the source agent's later high-recall
follow-up additions. Both invite fresh analysis. A suggestion is not
pre-accepted, and neither role nor its label may veto a grounded discovery.

When an authored row or reciprocal item has `source_target_is_range: true`, its
note was authored for the complete range. Exact Arabic for every component is
supplied in `source_target_range_evidence`. Test the exposed component on its
own wording, keep sequence-level material at range scope, and never attribute
another component's feature or morphology to it.

The supplied candidates are a review docket, not an accepted list and not a
quota. Review every candidate and the underlying wider evidence. You may recover
a missed finding when that evidence supports a materially distinct change in
the ayah's reading.

The packet's `hft_evidence.assigned_records` contain HFT nominations with a
wider-than-pericope anchor and individually split HFT reader-synthesis items;
each is a mandatory-review candidate with no presumptive outcome. Its exact raw
payload and exact available anchor Arabic are supplied by its sole `support_id`.
Review every one. Source binding, packet scope, confidence, or a missing
independent branch registry may limit certainty, but may never decide the
outcome. Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only; HFT-stated segmentation,
word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless
independently supplied. Treat unanchored synthesis as an inventory item to
trace, decompose, represent, refer, or reject—not as an acceptable surah thesis.
If you accept, narrow, refer, or represent an HFT item as an exact duplicate,
carry its support ID and preserve its source containment, live alternatives,
analogy limits, and any “not a lexical gloss” boundary. A duplicate target must
preserve the evidence union.

Do not search only for confirmation of supplied candidates. Independently scan
every wider-reading record for uncandidate discoveries and record every
meaningful contact opportunity. For every cited branch, explain the distinctive
facet that matters; a branch ID or generic gloss alone is false coverage.

Use the complete focus-branch atlas, not only candidate-cited branches. Silently
scan every atlas branch and every one of its supplied facets against the wider
records and supplied connections. A multi-facet branch is not reviewed by
choosing one representative facet. `review_facets` is the packet's normalized
authoritative facet list, including stable IDs for older source images that
lacked numbered concept-map facets. Record `atlas_facets_tested[]` for every
branch with a plausible wider contact or an existing citation in a supplied
candidate, an emitted contact, or an accepted finding, listing all tested facet
IDs and a branch-level `result` (`nominated`,
`no_independent_trigger`, or `scope_referral`), and any contact refs. The facet
statements stay in the packet and need not be copied into the response. Emit
findings only for real contacts; do not enumerate branch-by-branch combinations.
Every tested facet row is a `{facet_id, result}` object whose `result` is only
`contact`, `no_independent_trigger`, or `scope_referral`; a `nominated` branch
must land in an accepted finding or a linked
accepted/narrowed/represented/referred contact.

Every accepted finding must re-enter through a word, relation, or act already
present in the focus ayah. Reader agreement is not authority; an outlier is not
valuable merely because it is surprising. Provenance qualifications govern
`epistemic_status` and containment, not visibility. They do not override a
grounded return path, and they are not independent evidence that a reading is
false.

Reject only when you can name the failed edge: no ayah-local return path, no
wider trigger stated in the supplied record or freshly identifiable in the
exact supplied Arabic, no stated or freshly grounded relation, no changed
reading or payoff, an uncontained thesis, or an exact duplicate. Missing target
morphology is not by itself a rejection reason when the finding stays within
the exact supplied Arabic and a relation grounded by either the supplied note
or fresh focus-side assessment; do not invent lexical or morphological details.
Different or countervailing wider discoveries remain eligible.
For a duplicate, name the accepted target, explicitly show that local anchor,
mechanism, direction, and reader payoff are all the same, and preserve the
union of its evidence. Merely saying that it is already represented is
insufficient. Canonical status, source count, confidence labels, vividness, or
anticipated prose length never decides.
Scope overreach justifies rejection only when no bounded global core survives;
otherwise narrow the claim or issue a `scope_referral`.

Work in two internal phases. First answer the bold global question and discover
real carrier/trigger/relation/payoff returns without drafting coverage rows.
Then serialize those completed judgments. Coverage rows verify a judgment; they
must not generate one. Before returning JSON, re-ask the bold question and
recover any wider visibility that connection or facet accounting nearly
displaced.

Boundary example: valid coverage says that carrier `X`, facet `F002` (“a
boundary channels movement”), and a distant blocked destination together make
an otherwise generic motion read as constrained passage, with a stated reader
payoff. False coverage merely lists `root_x/B_y`, `F002`, or “boundary/path”
without the wider trigger, return path, and changed reading.

Do not write a surah thesis and do not write the commentary yet. Acceptance
must be settled before prose facility can influence it.

## Required response

Return one JSON object with:

All model-created stable refs in this response (`finding_ref`, `proposal_key`,
`contact_ref`, and `referral_ref`) must begin with `global:` so their identities
remain unambiguous when the three independent ledgers are reconciled.

- `schema_version: "commentary-v3-scope-review-v2"`;
- `identity` containing `ayah_ref: "1:6"`, `lane: "global"`,
  `lane_packet_sha256: "81a1be0f6673ef975e9ef7d4f10ca48a81ed79eb4a9aa12c87b32c100a84cbe9"`, and
  `authoring_request_sha256: "7c3df018894023b829f33b8904297df9a11bd225408c204766856b223ce94003"`;
- `ayah_ref: "1:6"` and `lane: "global"`;
- `coverage_complete`, true only after every supplied candidate, every assigned
  HFT record, and every supplied wider-reading record is reviewed;
- `support_coverage[]`, one row per supplied non-HFT wider-reading support
  record, with
  its exact `support_id`, `result` (`accepted`, `narrowed`, `represented`, or
  `rejected`), related `finding_refs`, and concise `reason`;
- `connection_coverage[]`, one row per supplied connection, with
  `connection_ref`, `target_ref`, `result` (`accepted`, `narrowed`,
  `represented`, or `rejected`), related `finding_refs`, and a specific reason
  independent of the prior label. Include `evidence_row_results[]`, exactly one
  row per supplied authored or reciprocal `connection_evidence_ref`, each with
  its own result from the same enum, related `finding_refs`, and a specific
  reason. The connection-level `finding_refs` are the exact union of the nested
  rows; its result is the first present result in this precedence: `accepted`,
  `narrowed`, `represented`, `rejected`;
- `atlas_facets_tested[]` as defined above, so a cited or plausible branch cannot
  hide behind a generic gloss or one representative facet;
- `contact_opportunities[]`, one row per meaningful wider contact, with stable
  `contact_ref`, `focus_carrier`, `wider_trigger`, `relation`,
  `isolated_before`, `wider_after`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`, complete
  `support_ids`, complete `branch_refs`, complete `connection_refs`,
  `disposition` (`accept`, `narrow`, `represented`, or `scope_referral`), the
  accepted `finding_refs` for an accepted, narrowed, or exactly represented
  contact, and any `cross_root_boundary` needed to prevent analogy from being
  mistaken for lexical identity. A failed attempted edge stays in candidate,
  connection, or facet coverage with its specific failure; it is not a contact
  and receives no `contact_ref`;
- `candidate_decisions[]`, one row per supplied candidate, with `candidate_id`,
  `decision` (`accept`, `narrow`, `reject`, or `scope_referral`), concise
  `reason`, `failed_edge` when rejected, related `accepted_finding_refs`, and
  nullable `duplicate_of`, complete `excluded_branch_refs`, and
  `branch_exclusion_reasons[]` (`branch_ref`, `reason`). Use empty arrays when no
  branch was dropped. Do not repeat the candidate's original evidence arrays;
  they remain available by `candidate_id` in the packet;
- `scope_referrals[]`, one row for every candidate decision, atlas row, or
  contact that uses `scope_referral`, with stable `referral_ref`, `origin_ref`,
  `referred_lane`, `focus_carrier`, `mechanism`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`,
  complete `support_ids`, `branch_refs`, `connection_refs`, and `contact_refs`,
  plus a specific `reason`. A referral is lossless work for another lane, not a
  rejection shorthand;
- `new_findings[]`, a compact map for uncandidate discoveries, each with a
  stable `proposal_key`, its `accepted_finding_ref`, a nonempty exact
  `contact_refs` list naming its originating contacts, and any originating
  `connection_refs`; put the finding content and evidence only in
  `accepted_findings`;
- `accepted_findings[]`, one row for every accepted existing or new finding,
  with `finding_ref`, complete `candidate_ids`, complete `proposal_keys`,
  `title`, `isolated_before`, `wider_after`, `wider_trigger`, `claim`,
  `mechanism`, `reader_payoff`, `containment`, complete `support_ids`,
  `branch_refs`, `connection_refs`, `contact_refs`, and `branch_contributions`,
  and `epistemic_status`. Supply one or more distinct contributions for every
  cited `branch_ref`; give separate rows when one branch activates multiple
  distinctive facets or independently anchored contributions, rather than
  flattening them. Each row states the exact single `facet_id` from that
  branch's `review_facets`, distinctive facet, carrier, independent anchor,
  actual contribution, and boundary. The same facet ID must have result
  `contact` in that branch's tested-facet ledger;
- `friction_notes[]` for live ambiguity, evidence gaps, source qualification,
  scope referrals, or decisions a reconciler should inspect.

Every supplied candidate must receive exactly one decision. Every accepted or
narrowed candidate must appear in `accepted_findings`. Referred and rejected
material remains visible in the decision ledger. Do not rank accepted findings.
Every assigned HFT record is already one supplied candidate; qualified metadata
cannot erase it.
An accepted finding cites only the support, branches, and connections its final
claim actually uses. When narrowing drops an original branch, record it in the
owning candidate decision's `excluded_branch_refs` and
`branch_exclusion_reasons`; do not preserve discarded branches as false semantic
coverage. Every `scope_referral` disposition must have one stable payload in
`scope_referrals`.
Semantic completeness matters more than cosmetic JSON regularity; never omit a
reading merely to simplify the response envelope.

After returning the decision ledger, remain in this same conversation. A later
follow-up will supply the reconciled, locked global set for prose preparation.

924c00831b29b518afb9c05e1a094a85866c4fd04071c","source_id":"quran-data/inter-ayah-row-reciprocal-v2"},"lane_branch_count":54,"lane_candidate_count":30,"lane_support_count":30,"missing_hft_anchor_refs":[],"quran_text_source":{"ayah_count":6348,"source_id":"quran-data/quran-uthmani","source_path":"quran-data/data/text/quran-uthmani.tsv","source_sha256":"9c89cdb0be69ced88ce38bae183b336ae6a6e4eed2f1ae3db0f7e811b8e729dd"},"source_types":["cross_run_publication","hft","v12_reader_walks","v12_reader_walks_wide"],"total_hft_record_count":25,"unresolved_branch_refs":[]},"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_001273/B008","root_001583/B001","root_001583/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_06a32012628ba40e4ec0","text":"1. **Guidance as leading from the front, not only informing.** Lexical evidence: ه د ي B001, gentle guidance toward way/truth; ه د ي B003, leading front part; ص ر ط B001, road; ق و م B008, straightness/uprightness. Structural cue: the imperative “guide us” asks for a road, not merely knowledge. Assigned role: هدى is the forward guide; صراط is the traversable field; مستقيم is the upright shape of movement. Abductive move: the packet supplies guide/front/road/straightness; I infer that guidance includes being led ahead. Reading change: the ayah reads as a request for conducted movement, not just instruction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B002","root_001583/B003","root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_1e59e853b42aa64bd41d","text":"The sole later ayah, 1:7, substantially changed the reading. Its appositional people-defined `صراط` and `أنعمت` activated fixed `ه د ي` B004 and B003: guidance is both gift and entry behind a leading human front. Its final `الضالين` also exposed the shared disappearance imagery of fixed `ص ر ط` B002 and `ض ل ل` B002–B003, correcting a static map-like reading into a contrast between contained transit and untracked loss.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001273/B004","root_001273/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_28fa7e1ce5d1e71926d8","text":"3. **Straightness as maintained and sustaining infrastructure.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ق و م` B004 is care, guardianship, and preservation, and B009 is the support or mainstay by which something stands and life is sustained. Earlier `ر ب ب` B002 supplies nurture and administration, and `ع و ن` B001 supplies backing. **Structural cue:** nurture, aid, and the `مستقيم` path form a causal sequence. **Role and abductive move:** the fixed branches make straightness both an effect of maintenance and a source of support; I infer that the route remains upright because it is sustained and that it in turn sustains its travelers. **Reading change:** `المستقيم` describes a dependable life-bearing order, not only a geometrically unbent line.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B004","root_001583/B005"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2f91411b70a44f111c02","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:6:1\",\"root_001583\",[\"B004\",\"B005\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Doğru yola iletilme, hak edilmiş bir yön bilgisi değil, yakınlık içinde bağışlanan ve insanı yola sokan bir armağan olarak görünür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000552/B002","root_001525/B001","root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_30668ee18a25dd761069","text":"4. **Guidance as gift/favor.** Lexical evidence: ه د ي B004, gracious gift sent to one close; ن ع م B001 in 1:7, bestowed favor; ر ح م B002/B003 in 1:1–1:3, kinship/womb relation. Structural cue: the next ayah identifies the desired path by those on whom “You bestowed favor.” Assigned role: guidance is a gracious transfer that places the receiver within a favored relation. Abductive move: the packet supplies gift, favor, and relational mercy; I infer that being guided is a received benefaction, not a self-generated achievement. Reading change: “Guide us” reads as “grant us the path as a favoring gift.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_3619f6287316e9e72dbb","text":"3. **The path as passage that can swallow, hide, or cut through.** Evidence: `ص ر ط B002` disappearing in passage/swallowing and `ص ر ط B003` cutting sword passing through, activated by `ض ل ل B002/B003` hiddenness/loss in 1:7 and `غ ض ب B004/B008` hard rock/tough hide. Mechanism: the requested path is not a neutral line; it is a passage through conditions where things disappear, are lost, or meet hard resistance. The packet supplies passage/swallowing, cutting-through, hidden loss, and hard compactness; I infer a dangerous transit mechanism. Abductive move: straightness matters because passage itself can consume or deflect the traveler. Reading change: الصراط المستقيم becomes a protected passage through risk, not only a morally correct road.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000913/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_366d4de2b243c775b9c4","text":"1:7 most strongly changed this ayah: the repeated صراط revealed that “straight” is populated by a history of favored people and defined by avoided failure-modes. Also, ض ل ل B002 made ص ر ط B002 surprising: a path can become a swallowing disappearance unless upheld and guided.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001064/B001","root_001273/B008","root_001583/B003","root_001583/B008"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_3d28c149da1f4b3415cc","text":"2. **Guidance as a supported procession.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ه د ي` B003 is the leading front part or guide going ahead, and B008 is supported, swaying walking; fixed `ق و م` B008 supplies upright alignment. Preceding `ع و ن` B001 supplies requested support, and 1:7 supplies people already associated with the path. **Structural cue:** aid comes first, then guidance, then the human exemplars who are ahead on the road. **Role and abductive move:** the earlier recipients function as a leading front and aid steadies those who would otherwise sway; I infer both roles from the ordered sequence. **Reading change:** guidance becomes accompanied movement into an existing procession, not information delivered to an already stable traveler.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000532/B002","root_001273/B004","root_001273/B009","root_001444/B005"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_41f048eb532804669bd7","text":"2. **The straight path as maintained support.** Lexical evidence: ق و م B004, care/guardianship/preservation; ق و م B009, support/mainstay/subsistence; م ل ك B005 in 1:4, mainstay; ر ب ب B002 in 1:2, nurturing/completion. Structural cue: مستقيم is built from ق و م and appears after Lordship, ownership, worship, and help. Assigned role: straightness is the maintained condition by which the path keeps standing. Abductive move: the packet supplies nurture, mainstay, support, and straightness; I infer that the path remains straight because it is upheld, not because it is merely geometrically direct. Reading change: “straight” reads as sustained uprightness and managed stability.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B002","root_000913/B002","root_000913/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_49a5053e730283075769","text":"3. **The path as passage that can swallow the traveler.** Lexical evidence: ص ر ط B002, disappearing in passage and swallowing; ض ل ل B002 in 1:7, hiddenness/disappearance; ض ل ل B003, loss. Structural cue: the same صراط is repeated in 1:7 and then contrasted with lost/astray groups. Assigned role: صراط carries an ambivalent passage-image: movement can be safe traversal or disappearance. Abductive move: the packet supplies swallowing and later disappearance/loss; I infer that the prayer asks for a passage that does not erase or lose the traveler. Reading change: the road becomes perilous and absorbing, not merely a clear line.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_49e4d38c1b71ef25496e","text":"4. **Guidance as gift and offering.** Evidence: `ه د ي B004` gracious gift, `ه د ي B005` offering sent to sanctuary, activated by `ن ع م B001` bestowed favor in 1:7 and `ح م د B001` thanksgiving in 1:2. Mechanism: the request for guidance receives the gift-field of هدي: the path is something graciously sent or given, and the response to it is praise. The packet supplies gift/offering, favor, and thanksgiving; I infer a reciprocal devotional economy. Abductive move: what guides is not earned by the speaker’s service; it is bestowed, then answered by praise and worship. Reading change: “اهدنا” becomes request for a gift-bearing guidance, not a claim to entitlement.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_59a515597afbe00023f0","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:6:2\",\"root_000858\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yol, yolcuyu belirli bir geçiş içinde taşıyan koruyucu bir kanal olur; bunun karşıtı, izlenebilir bir güzergâhın dışında kaybolmaktır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B004","root_001273/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_6a8dd4a4e9cd9a825e5e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:6:3\",\"root_001273\",[\"B004\",\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yolun doğruluğu, yalnız geometrik düzlük değil, bakımla ayakta tutulan ve yürüyenleri de ayakta tutan güvenilir bir düzen niteliğidir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B003","root_001583/B008"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_736ba8ea41445bc81d9f","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:6:1\",\"root_001583\",[\"B003\",\"B008\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Yol gösterme, uzaktan bilgi vermekten çok, önden gidenlerin ardından desteklenerek ve dengede tutularak yürütülme biçimi kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B002","root_000913/B002","root_000913/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_83fe8eaf2117825c216a","text":"4. **An exploratory contrast between contained passage and untracked disappearance.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ص ر ط` B002 is disappearance through passage or swallowing; later `ض ل ل` B002 is hidden disappearance and B003 is loss of locatability. **Structural cue:** `صراط` is repeated at the start of 1:7 before the contrasting `الضالين`. **Role and abductive move:** both sides supply disappearance images, but I infer a directional distinction: the road contains and carries passage, whereas straying removes the traveler from a locatable course. **Reading change:** the straight path can absorb the traveler into disciplined transit, contrasted with being absorbed into trackless loss.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_9fa85b7808c7a8630fee","text":"1:7 made this ayah more concrete: الصراط is immediately restated as the path of a group, so guidance is social and historical, not only abstract direction. The negative end of 1:7 also activated `ص ر ط B002`; a path can become disappearance if it is not مستقيم, which made “straight” feel protective rather than merely geometrical.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001273/B003","root_001583/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_a792f5ad76372336ee13","text":"2. **A leading front, not only an abstract direction.** Evidence: `ه د ي B003` leading front part/guide, activated by `م ل ك B008` leading forepart in animals and by `ص ر ط B001` road; supported by `ق و م B003` resolve/rising to undertake a matter. Mechanism: guidance becomes embodied as something or someone going ahead; straightness is followed through a leading edge. The packet supplies leading-part branches and the road; I infer a caravan-like directionality. Abductive move: those who ask “guide us” need a front to follow, not only a map. Reading change: “اهدنا” becomes a request to be led from ahead into undertaking, not only to be instructed from a distance.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001583/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d0e77c765a01f0e2ea6b","text":"1. **Gentle showing of the straight road.** Evidence: `ه د ي B001` gentle guidance toward way/truth, `ص ر ط B001` straight road, and `ق و م B008` straightness/uprightness/evenness. Mechanism: the three roots form the surface mechanism: an addressee is asked to show a road whose defining quality is uprightness. The packet supplies guidance, road, and straightness; I infer that guidance is not only information but movement toward an aligned course. Abductive move: a straight path must be both indicated and sustained, because the speaker cannot generate it by worship alone. Reading change: the ayah reads as a request for enabled orientation, not merely for knowledge of a route.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_f43b158ed49bdefbdfcf","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"1:6:2\",\"root_000858\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Doğru yol, sertleşmiş dirence takılmadan içinden geçebilen kesici bir geçit boyutu da kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000858/B001","root_001583/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fa2369a343ce80ae1f2c","text":"1. **Guidance as a bestowed gift.** **Branch evidence:** fixed `ه د ي` B004 is a gracious gift sent to one who is close, while 1:7 `ن ع م` B001 is bestowed favor; fixed `ص ر ط` B001 supplies the road. **Structural cue:** the petition is immediately expanded as the road of people upon whom the addressee has conferred favor. **Role and abductive move:** the gift branch supplies mode of transfer and the next ayah supplies benefaction and recipients; I infer that favor takes concrete form as access to the road. **Reading change:** `اهدنا` is not merely “give us directions” but “bestow the traversable way upon us.”","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001064/B001","root_001583/B010"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:5","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ff64000afd93fc27777d","text":"5. **Guidance as composed, non-panicked bearing.** Lexical evidence: ه د ي B010, calm stillness and composed bearing; ع و ن B001 in 1:5, help/backing; غ ض ب B001 and ض ل ل B001 in 1:7, anger and deviation. Structural cue: the request for help precedes the request for guidance, and the following ayah names destabilizing alternatives. Assigned role: الهداية steadies the petitioner against panic, anger, and drift. Abductive move: the packet supplies calm bearing and later destabilizing states; I infer that guidance has an affective-postural dimension. Reading change: the ayah asks for a composed way of moving, not only the correct route.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
